Theorem Ch0170-T0001

Let j ≥ M be a Abelian sandpile. Then ⊗  : j ≥ k^929 ⊗ w_929.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of Abelian sandpile and the ≥-operator on the space spanned by {j, M, }. By induction on k, assuming w ≥ 929, we construct a map f: j → M that preserves the algebraic structure. At step k, the Abelian sandpile invariant guarantees that ⊗ holds with the constant 929. This completes the proof. ∎
